    // Hide Header on on scroll down
    var didScroll ;
    var lastScrollTop = 0;
    var delta = 5;
    var navbarHeight = jQuery('.page-header').outerHeight();
    jQuery(window).scroll(function(event){
        didScroll = true;
    });
    setInterval(function() {
        if (didScroll) {
            hasScrolled();
            didScroll = false;
        }
    }, 250);
    function hasScrolled() {
        var st = jQuery(this).scrollTop();
        if(Math.abs(lastScrollTop - st) <= delta)
            return;
        if (st > lastScrollTop && st > navbarHeight){
            // Scroll Down
        	jQuery('.page-header').removeClass('nav-down').addClass('nav-up');
        } else {
            // Scroll Up
            if(st + jQuery(window).height() < jQuery(document).height()) {
            	jQuery('.page-header').removeClass('nav-up').addClass('nav-down');
            }
        }
        lastScrollTop = st;
    }
    //]]>

    function getIEVersion(){
      var agent = navigator.userAgent;
      var reg = /MSIE\s?(\d+)(?:\.(\d+))?/i;
      var matches = agent.match(reg);
      if (matches != null) {
        return { major: matches[1], minor: matches[2] };
      }
      return { major: "-1", minor: "-1" };

        function getContentFinalHeight(){
        	var width = jQuery(window).width();
            var fullHeight = 600;
            var fullWidth = 1500;
            if(width >= 1500){
            	return fullHeight;
        	}else {
        		return (fullHeight * width) / fullWidth;
        	}
        }
        function setContentHeights(){
        	height = getContentFinalHeight();
        	var thisExtendedContent = jQuery('#widget-id-2045');
        	thisExtendedContent.outerHeight(height);
        	thisExtendedContent.css("min-height", height);
        	thisExtendedContent.find('.extendedcontent').each(function(){
    			jQuery(this).outerHeight(height);
    			jQuery(this).find('.extendedcontent-col').each(function(){
    				jQuery(this).outerHeight(height);
    			});
    		});
        }
</script>

        function getContentFinalHeight(){
        	var width = jQuery(window).width();
            var fullHeight = 600;
            var fullWidth = 1500;
            if(width >= 1500){
            	return fullHeight;
        	}else {
        		return (fullHeight * width) / fullWidth;
        	}
        }
        function setContentHeights(){
        	height = getContentFinalHeight();
        	var thisExtendedContent = jQuery('#widget-id-1710');
        	thisExtendedContent.outerHeight(height);
        	thisExtendedContent.css("min-height", height);
        	thisExtendedContent.find('.extendedcontent').each(function(){
    			jQuery(this).outerHeight(height);
    			jQuery(this).find('.extendedcontent-col').each(function(){
    				jQuery(this).outerHeight(height);

    		});
        }
